(base a48c35f = main at 0.36.0, head ecd4362) on branch mirror-parent of
this repository, a Claude Code plugin whose tools are PowerShell scripts
under tools/ and whose evals are pytest modules under evals/. The range
implements the plan docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-13-mirror-parent.md,
whose section "Decisions the handoff left open, settled here" is the
binding design authority (there is no separate spec; the plan extends the
identity guard of docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-13-mirror-reaper-design.md
with a seventh rule). The plan was NOT debated before the build: the user
directed it from a KitnEssentials handoff, so this diff debate is the first
cross-vendor gate on the work, and a plan defect is as much a finding as an
implementation defect. BACKLOG.md item 107, follow-up 2, is the record of
why the work exists; follow-ups 1 and 3 of that item are out of scope.

What the range does, in one paragraph: review mirrors (a file copy of the
reviewed repository built by tools/new-review-mirror.ps1 for you to read;
you are reading one now) and the clone bridges beside them used to be built
under the controller host's temp directory, and the KitnEssentials sessions
built them at the drive root because a 36-character temp path blew a
15-character budget, which is how 78 directories (13.4 GB) came to sit at
C:\ on 2026-09-13. The declaration's review-mirror row now names a fixed
parent, `C:/pxm/<short-name>/`. tools/artifact-roots.ps1, the one reader of
that declaration, stops substituting the temp directory and answers
`-Assert <path> -Expect reviewMirror` for a path before a mirror is built.
tools/write-attestation.ps1 reads the parent through that tool, in-process,
and adds one LAST rule to its reap identity guard: a tree named as
-ReapMirror or -ReapBridge must sit under the parent (any depth, never the
parent itself), or the record is not written. Doctor check 10 inventories
the parent as a directory and keeps the old drive-root name sweep as a
legacy line. The skill prose names the parent. The tests build every tree a
success case names under a per-test directory of the REAL parent,
C:\pxm\t-<8 hex>, and remove it.

The required whole-branch review from the same-vendor fable-reviewer seat
ran on a48c35f..2df3d45 and is retained verbatim at
C:\Temp\parallax-scratch\2026-09-13-mirror-parent\fable-diff-r1-reply.md
(outside this working directory; its findings are summarized under claim
8). It returned Ready to merge: With fixes, one Important and three Minor;
every accepted finding was applied at 3032444, and a re-review of that fix
wave found all addressed. That review is input, never authority.

The diff of the code, test, skill, command and backlog surfaces is appended
to this brief under <diff>; the plan is in this working directory, which
carries the branch's .git, so `git diff a48c35f..HEAD` and `git log` are
available to you.</task>

+@needs_host
+def test_expect_review_mirror_answers_for_the_declared_parent(tmp_path):
+    # The check a session runs BEFORE building a mirror (preflight-mirror.md):
+    # the declared parent accepts a direct child and a deeper path, and
+    # refuses the two places mirrors used to be built, the host temp
+    # directory and the drive root. The paths need not exist.
+    repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
+    for inside in (r"C:\pxm\kv-t", "C:/pxm/kvs-t", r"C:\pxm\t-1\kv-t"):
+        proc = run_resolver("-RepoRoot", str(repo), "-Assert", inside,
+                            "-Expect", "reviewMirror")
+        assert proc.returncode == 0, inside + ": " + proc.stdout + proc.stderr
+        assert "assert: inside review mirror root:" in proc.stdout, proc.stdout
+    temp_root = os.environ.get("TEMP") or tmp_path
+    for outside in (str(Path(temp_root) / "kv-t"), r"C:\kv-t", r"C:\pxmx\kv-t"):
+        proc = run_resolver("-RepoRoot", str(repo), "-Assert", outside,
+                            "-Expect", "reviewMirror")
+        assert proc.returncode == 1, outside + ": " + proc.stdout + proc.stderr
+        assert "outside every retained root" in proc.stdout, proc.stdout
+    # The parent itself is never a tree: outside for the mirror row, the
+    # same answer the emitter's reap guard gives, so the pre-build check
+    # and the reap agree.
+    for parent in (r"C:\pxm", "C:/pxm/"):
+        proc = run_resolver("-RepoRoot", str(repo), "-Assert", parent,
+                            "-Expect", "reviewMirror")
+        assert proc.returncode == 1, parent + ": " + proc.stdout + proc.stderr
+        assert "outside every retained root" in proc.stdout, proc.stdout
+    got = json.loads(run_resolver("-RepoRoot", str(repo), "-Assert", r"C:\pxm\kv-t",
+                                  "-Expect", "reviewMirror", "-Json").stdout)
+    assert got["assert"] == {"path": "C:/pxm/kv-t", "inside": True,
+                             "root": "review mirror root",
+                             "expected": "review mirror root"}

+MIRROR_ROW = re.compile(r"^Canonical review mirror root: `([^`<]+)<short-name>/`$", re.M)
+PARENT_SPELLING = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z]:[/\\]+pxm(?![A-Za-z0-9_.-])", re.I)
+
+
+def declared_mirror_parent():
+    """The parent the declaration names, normalized: `C:/pxm/<short-name>/`
+    gives `c:/pxm`."""
+    m = MIRROR_ROW.search(read(NOTES))
+    assert m, "the review mirror row is not in the declared shape"
+    return norm(m.group(1))
+
+
+def test_every_parent_spelling_on_the_surface_is_the_declared_one():
+    # Prose and the doctor NAME the parent as an example (`C:\pxm\kv-<tag>`).
+    # An example is not a second declaration only while it agrees with
+    # the row: every drive-rooted `pxm` spelling on the plugin surface is
+    # the declared parent, so a renamed row turns each stale example red.
+    parent = declared_mirror_parent()
+    assert parent == "c:/pxm", parent
+    seen = 0
+    for pattern in PLUGIN_SURFACE:
+        for f in sorted(REPO.glob(pattern)):
+            if not f.is_file():
+                continue
+            for lineno, line in enumerate(read(f).splitlines(), 1):
+                for m in PARENT_SPELLING.finditer(line):
+                    seen += 1
+                    assert re.sub(r"[/\\]+", "/", m.group(0)).lower() == parent, (
+                        f"{f.relative_to(REPO).as_posix()}:{lineno} names "
+                        f"{m.group(0)}, not the declared parent")
+    assert seen >= 4, "the notes, the prose examples and the doctor should name the parent"
+
+
+def test_parent_spelling_regex_can_fail():
+    assert PARENT_SPELLING.search(r"build at C:\pxm\kv-<tag>")
+    assert PARENT_SPELLING.search("C:/pxm/<short-name>/")
+    assert not PARENT_SPELLING.search(r"C:\pxmx\kv-t")
+    assert not PARENT_SPELLING.search("the pxm parent")
+    assert norm(PARENT_SPELLING.search(r"D:\PXM\x").group(0)) == "d:/pxm"
+    assert not PARENT_SPELLING.search(r"C:\pxm.old")
+    assert PARENT_SPELLING.search(r"C:\\pxm\\x")
+

+# The DECLARED review mirror parent (model-prompting-notes.md,
+# round-artifact-roots, `Canonical review mirror root`). The emitter reaps
+# nothing outside it, so every tree a success case names is built under
+# a per-test directory here and removed afterwards. The literal is a pin:
+# test_artifact_roots.py binds the resolver's answer to the row.
+PARENT = Path(r"C:\pxm")
+
+
+def _clear_readonly_and_retry(func, path, exc):
+    os.chmod(path, stat.S_IWRITE)
+    func(path)
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def pxm():
+    """A fresh `C:\\pxm\\t-<8 hex>` for one test, removed on the way out
+    whatever the test left behind (a held-handle case leaves its trees).
+    Git's read-only object files are made writable as they are hit.
+    Nothing else under the parent is ever touched."""
+    d = PARENT / ("t-" + uuid.uuid4().hex[:8])
+    d.mkdir(parents=True)
+    yield d
+    if d.exists():
+        shutil.rmtree(d, onexc=_clear_readonly_and_retry)
+

+def doctored_plugin(tmp_path, replacement):
+    """A copy of the three attestation-side tools beside a doctored notes
+    file, so a parent-read failure can be driven without touching the real
+    declaration. `replacement` is what the review mirror row becomes; an
+    empty string removes the row."""
+    fake = tmp_path / "plugin"
+    (fake / "tools").mkdir(parents=True)
+    notes_dir = fake / "skills" / "multi-model-verify" / "references"
+    notes_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
+    for name in ("write-attestation.ps1", "artifact-roots.ps1", "review-tree-removal.ps1"):
+        shutil.copy(REPO / "tools" / name, fake / "tools" / name)
+    notes = REPO / "skills" / "multi-model-verify" / "references" / "model-prompting-notes.md"
+    row = "Canonical review mirror root: `C:/pxm/<short-name>/`\n"
+    assert row in read(notes)
+    (notes_dir / "model-prompting-notes.md").write_text(
+        read(notes).replace(row, replacement), encoding="utf-8")
+    return fake / "tools" / "write-attestation.ps1"
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
+# Group 7: the declared mirror parent (item 107, follow-up 2)
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
+def test_a_tree_outside_the_declared_parent_is_refused_for_mirror_and_bridge(tmp_path):
+    # The LAST rule of the identity guard: a mirror and a bridge that
+    # pass every other rule, built under the host temp directory as every
+    # tree was until 2026-09-13, are refused by name with the record
+    # unwritten. The message names the declared parent.
+    repo, base, head = make_repo(tmp_path)
+    mirror = make_mirror(repo, tmp_path / "kv-t")
+    bridge = make_bridge(repo, tmp_path / "kvs-t")
+    for kwargs, label in (({"mirror": mirror}, "the reap mirror"),
+                          ({"bridge": bridge}, "the reap bridge")):
+        proc = attest(repo, base, head, **kwargs)
+        assert proc.returncode == 2, label + ": " + proc.stdout + proc.stderr
+        assert ("ERROR: " + label + " is not under the declared review mirror parent C:/pxm (") in proc.stdout, proc.stdout
+        assert not att_file(repo, head).exists()
+    assert mirror.exists() and bridge.exists()
+
+
+def test_the_parent_itself_is_never_a_reap_path():
+    # Source pin: the comparison is StartsWith on the parent WITH its
+    # separator, never Equals, so `C:\pxm` can never be named as a tree.
+    body = read(WRITE)
+    guard = body[body.index("function Resolve-ReapPath"):body.index("function Invoke-Reap")]
+    assert '$parentSlash = $mirrorParent.TrimEnd("/") + "/"' in guard
+    assert "$p.StartsWith($parentSlash, $cmp)" in guard
+    assert "$p.Equals($parentSlash, $cmp)" in guard
+    # And it is the LAST rule: the head comparison precedes it.
+    assert guard.index("not the attested head") < guard.index("not under the declared review mirror parent")
+
+
+def test_an_unreadable_parent_refuses_every_reap(tmp_path, pxm):
+    # The emitter reads the parent through artifact-roots.ps1, the one
+    # reader of the declaration. A copy of the three tools beside a
+    # doctored notes file whose mirror row has no placeholder cannot
+    # resolve a parent, and a parent that cannot be read accepts
+    # NOTHING: exit 2, record unwritten, tree untouched - even for a tree
+    # that sits under the real parent.
+    emitter = doctored_plugin(tmp_path, "Canonical review mirror root: `C:/pxm/`\n")
+    repo, base, head = make_repo(tmp_path)
+    mirror = make_mirror(repo, pxm / "kv-t")
+    proc = run_ps(emitter,
+                  "-RepoRoot", str(repo), "-BaseSha", base, "-HeadSha", head,
+                  "-Verdict", "PASS", "-VerificationStatus", "FULL",
+                  "-RouteNote", "effective route confirmed", "-Rounds", "1",
+                  "-Participants", "session/reviewer", "-ReapMirror", str(mirror))
+    assert proc.returncode == 2, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
+    assert "ERROR: the declared review mirror parent could not be read" in proc.stdout, proc.stdout
+    assert not att_file(repo, head).exists()
+    assert mirror.exists()
+    # Without a reap parameter the same doctored copy never reads the
+    # parent and writes the record as before.
+    proc = run_ps(emitter,
+                  "-RepoRoot", str(repo), "-BaseSha", base, "-HeadSha", head,
+                  "-Verdict", "PASS", "-VerificationStatus", "FULL",
+                  "-RouteNote", "effective route confirmed", "-Rounds", "1",
+                  "-Participants", "session/reviewer")
+    assert proc.returncode == 0, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
+    assert att_file(repo, head).is_file()
+
+
+def test_a_roots_tool_that_exits_nonzero_refuses_every_reap(tmp_path, pxm):
+    # The other failure direction of the parent read: the doctored notes

-function Resolve-ReapPath($label, $raw, $repoTop, $commonFull, $headFull, $allowRemediation) {
+function Resolve-MirrorParent($repoRoot) {
+    # The declared review mirror parent, read through the ONE reader of
+    # the round-artifact-roots declaration, tools/artifact-roots.ps1,
+    # invoked in-process so its exit reaches $LASTEXITCODE here (the
+    # tool's documented $args fallback; measured 2026-09-13 on both
+    # hosts). Every failure exits 2 with nothing written: a parent that
+    # could not be read is never a parent that accepts everything.
+    $tool = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot "artifact-roots.ps1"
+    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $tool -PathType Leaf)) {
+        Write-Output "ERROR: the declared review mirror parent could not be read: $tool is missing"
+        exit 2
+    }
+    $lines = @()
+    $why = ""
+    try {
+        $lines = @(& $tool -RepoRoot $repoRoot -Json 2>&1)
+    } catch {
+        $why = $_.Exception.Message
+    }
+    $toolExit = $LASTEXITCODE
+    $text = (@($lines | ForEach-Object { [string]$_ }) -join "`n")
+    if ($why) {
+        Write-Output ("ERROR: the declared review mirror parent could not be read: " + $why)
+        exit 2
+    }
+    if ($toolExit -ne 0) {
+        Write-Output ("ERROR: the declared review mirror parent could not be read: artifact-roots.ps1 exited " + $toolExit + ": " + $text)
+        exit 2
+    }
+    $row = ""
+    try {
+        $parsed = ConvertFrom-Json $text
+        $row = [string]$parsed.reviewMirror
+    } catch {
+        $row = ""
+    }
+    if (-not $row) {
+        Write-Output ("ERROR: the declared review mirror parent could not be read: no reviewMirror row in the tool's answer: " + $text)
+        exit 2
+    }
+    $i = $row.IndexOf("<")
+    if ($i -le 0) {
+        Write-Output ("ERROR: the declared review mirror parent could not be read: the row has no per-debate placeholder (" + $row + ")")
+        exit 2
+    }
+    $parent = $row.Substring(0, $i).Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/")
+    if ((-not $parent) -or (-not [System.IO.Path]::IsPathRooted($parent))) {
+        Write-Output ("ERROR: the declared review mirror parent could not be read: the row is not rooted (" + $row + ")")
+        exit 2
+    }
+    return $parent
+}

5: **Goal:** Review mirrors and clone bridges are built under ONE declared, fixed, short parent, `C:\pxm\<tag>`, instead of the controller host's temp directory or the drive root; every reader of the location (the declaration, its resolver, the attestation emitter's reap guard, the doctor and the skill prose) uses that parent, and the emitter refuses to reap a tree that is not under it.
6: 
7: **Architecture:** The `round-artifact-roots` declaration in `skills/multi-model-verify/references/model-prompting-notes.md` changes its review-mirror row to `C:/pxm/<short-name>/`. `tools/artifact-roots.ps1` stays the ONE reader: it stops substituting `<TEMP>`, gains `-Expect reviewMirror`, and puts the mirror row in its `-Assert` set. `tools/write-attestation.ps1` reads the parent THROUGH that reader (in-process, so the exit code reaches it) and adds one last identity-guard rule to `Resolve-ReapPath`, for the mirror AND the bridge: the tree must sit under the parent. `commands/doctor.md` check 10 inventories the parent as a directory and keeps the `kv*` drive-root sweep as a legacy line. The prose in SKILL.md, preflight-mirror.md and backup-lane.md names the parent. Tests: `test_artifact_roots.py` pins the row, the resolver's answer and a sweep shape for the retired `<TEMP>` form, and binds every `C:\pxm` spelling on the plugin surface to the declaration; `test_mirror_reaper.py` builds every tree a success case names under a per-test directory of the real parent and adds the refusal cases.
8: 
9: **Tech Stack:** PowerShell 5.1 and 7 (ASCII-only tools, LF), Python 3.12 pytest evals, GitHub Actions `powershell-hosts` job.
10: 
11: **Source:** BACKLOG item 107, follow-up 2 only; the handoff `C:\Users\Brandon\Documents\KitnDev\KitnEssentials\dev\docs\handoffs\parallax-mirror-parent-handoff.md`; the reap guard this extends is `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-13-mirror-reaper-design.md`, "The identity guard". The parent name `C:\pxm` was chosen by the user (2026-09-13). This plan was NOT debated before the build: the user directed it from the handoff, as with 0.36.0, so the mode-diff debate is the first cross-vendor gate on the work.
12: 
13: ## Decisions the handoff left open, settled here
14: 
15: 1. **The declared row is drive-rooted and fixed: `C:/pxm/<short-name>/`.** Forward slashes like every other row; the resolver normalizes. It is not derived from `SystemDrive`: the row is a declaration, one edit swaps it, and every reader parses it. Stated limit: a machine whose system drive is not `C:` edits the row.
16: 2. **"Under the parent" means any depth below it, never the parent itself.** The emitter compares `<full>/` against `C:/pxm/` with `OrdinalIgnoreCase` (`StartsWith`, not `Equals`), the same form the overlap comparison at `tools/write-attestation.ps1:123` uses, and the resolver's `-Assert -Expect reviewMirror` answers the same way for the parent itself (outside), a deviation from the other rows corrected 2026-09-13 after the Fable review. A session that groups its trees in a subdirectory of the parent can still reap them; the declaration's SHAPE is one segment, and preflight-mirror.md says so.
17: 3. **The rule covers `-ReapBridge`.** The bridge is built beside the mirror (`C:\pxm\kvs-<tag>`), the doctor counts it in the same inventory, and a bridge left outside the parent is exactly a tree the reaper should not touch by name.
18: 4. **The emitter reads the parent through `tools/artifact-roots.ps1`, never by its own literal.** Item 100 exists because each writer read the location on its own. The resolver runs in-process (`& <tool> -RepoRoot <repo> -Json`), which the tool supports through its documented `$args` fallback, so `exit 2` inside it reaches `$LASTEXITCODE` in the emitter (measured 2026-09-13 on both hosts); any failure to read the parent exits 2 with nothing written. The read happens only when a reap parameter is given, so an emitter run without `-ReapMirror`/`-ReapBridge` never touches the resolver.
19: 5. **The guard is the LAST rule in `Resolve-ReapPath`.** Every existing refusal fires first with its own message, so the existing refusal tests keep their trees in `tmp_path` and still name their reason; the parent guard is proven by trees that pass every earlier rule.
20: 6. **The mirror tool does not enforce the parent.** `tools/new-review-mirror.ps1` takes `-MirrorPath` and does not read the declaration; its callers include every test fixture that builds a mirror in a temporary directory. The check before a build is `tools/artifact-roots.ps1 -RepoRoot <repo> -Assert <mirror-path> -Expect reviewMirror`, run by the session (corrected 2026-09-13 after the Fable fix-wave re-review: this copy of the command had also omitted `-RepoRoot <repo>`); the check that matters is the emitter's, because the removal is the dangerous act. A mirror built elsewhere is refused at reap and removed by hand, which the prose says.
21: 7. **`<TEMP>` leaves the resolver entirely**, with its forbidden-character screen and the test that drove it, because no row names it any more; the sweep gains a shape for `<TEMP>` so the form cannot come back.
22: 8. **Item 107 goes PARTIAL**, follow-up 2 closed with the decision and the four edits recorded; follow-ups 1 and 3 stay in a `**What remains.**` paragraph.
23: 
24: ## Global Constraints
25: 
26: - Tools under `tools/` are ASCII ONLY, LF, and run under BOTH Windows PowerShell 5.1 and PowerShell 7. Never run a native `git` call under `$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'`; read `$LASTEXITCODE` yourself.
27: - `SKILL.md` is at 6492 of `skill_lint.py`'s 6500-token ceiling (`len(body) // 4` over the frontmatter-stripped body, measured 2026-09-13). Task 3 makes EXACTLY the one edit it quotes in that file (+8 characters). After the edit run `python evals/tools/skill_lint.py skills/multi-model-verify --strict`; an ERROR line means STOP and report, never trim other text.
28: - Do not reflow any existing paragraph in `skills/`, `agents/` or `commands/`: raw-text pins in `evals/` break on a rewrap. Insert whole new paragraphs or sentences; edit only the text a task quotes. In particular `never inside the reviewed repository` (model-prompting-notes.md) must stay whole on one physical line, and the eight declaration lines are pinned as raw text on eight physical lines.
29: - Stage by explicit path (`git add <file> <file>`); `git add -A` is refused by the family git guard. Commit messages are lowercase imperative, no AI attribution, and must not contain a token that looks like a PowerShell flag (write "the expect parameter", never `-Expect`; "the reap mirror parameter", never `-ReapMirror`).
30: - Tests select the host through `PARALLAX_PS_HOST`. Before calling a task done, run its PowerShell-facing module under BOTH hosts, in PowerShell: `$env:PARALLAX_PS_HOST = "powershell.exe"; python -m pytest <module> -q` and then the same with `"pwsh.exe"`.
31: - Never touch the eight directories `C:\kv-bl-*` and `C:\kvs-bl-*`, nor the three directories that already sit under `C:\pxm` (`8904109a`, `k10392`, `k92104`; other chats own them). Every test builds its own trees under pytest's `tmp_path` or under a per-test directory `C:\pxm\t-<8 hex>` that the test's fixture creates and removes.
32: - The worktree is `C:\Users\Brandon\Documents\_worktrees\parallax-mirror-parent` on branch `mirror-parent`; run every command from there. The primary checkout at `C:\Users\Brandon\Documents\parallax` is the session's: never write there.
33: - Full gate before the Fable review (the session runs it, not a task): the six CI commands in `CLAUDE.md`, under both hosts.

310: ### Task 2: The emitter's parent guard and the reaper tests
311: 
312: **Files:**
313: - Modify: `tools/write-attestation.ps1`

316: **Interfaces:**
317: - Produces: `Resolve-MirrorParent($repoRoot)` in `tools/write-attestation.ps1`, returning the declared parent as a forward-slash path with no trailing separator (`C:/pxm`) or printing `ERROR: the declared review mirror parent could not be read ...` and exiting 2. `Resolve-ReapPath` gains a seventh, LAST parameter `$mirrorParent`; a tree not under it is refused with `ERROR: <label> is not under the declared review mirror parent <parent> (<full>)`, exit 2, nothing written. 320: - [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**
321: 
322: In `evals/multi-model-verify/test_mirror_reaper.py`:
323: 
324: (a) Add `import stat` and `import uuid` to the imports. After the `read` helper, add:
325: ```python
326: # The DECLARED review mirror parent (model-prompting-notes.md,
327: # round-artifact-roots, `Canonical review mirror root`). The emitter reaps
328: # nothing outside it, so every tree a success case names is built under
329: # a per-test directory here and removed afterwards. The literal is a pin:
330: # test_artifact_roots.py binds the resolver's answer to the row.
331: PARENT = Path(r"C:\pxm")
332: 
333: 
334: def _clear_readonly_and_retry(func, path, exc):
335:     os.chmod(path, stat.S_IWRITE)
336:     func(path)
337: 
338: 
339: @pytest.fixture
340: def pxm():
341:     """A fresh `C:\\pxm\\t-<8 hex>` for one test, removed on the way out
342:     whatever the test left behind (a held-handle case leaves its trees).
343:     Git's read-only object files are made writable as they are hit.
344:     Nothing else under the parent is ever touched."""
345:     d = PARENT / ("t-" + uuid.uuid4().hex[:8])
346:     d.mkdir(parents=True)
347:     yield d
348:     if d.exists():
349:         shutil.rmtree(d, onexc=_clear_readonly_and_retry)
350: ```
351:

456: #
457: # PARENT (0.37.0, backlog item 107): both trees must also sit under the
458: # review mirror parent the round-artifact-roots declaration names,
459: # read here through tools/artifact-roots.ps1 rather than by a literal

462: # keeps its own message.
463: ```
464: 
465: (b) After `Resolve-FullSha`, before `Resolve-ReapPath`, add:
466: ```powershell
467: function Resolve-MirrorParent($repoRoot) {
468:     # The declared review mirror parent, read through the ONE reader of
469:     # the round-artifact-roots declaration, tools/artifact-roots.ps1,
470:     # invoked in-process so its exit reaches $LASTEXITCODE here (the
471:     # tool's documented $args fallback; measured 2026-09-13 on both
472:     # hosts). Every failure exits 2 with nothing written: a parent that
473:     # could not be read is never a parent that accepts everything.
474:     $tool = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot "artifact-roots.ps1"
475:     if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $tool -PathType Leaf)) {
476:         Write-Output "ERROR: the declared review mirror parent could not be read: $tool is missing"
477:         exit 2
478:     }
479:     $lines = @()
480:     $why = ""
481:     try {
482:         $lines = @(& $tool -RepoRoot $repoRoot -Json 2>&1)
483:     } catch {
484:         $why = $_.Exception.Message
485:     }
486:     $toolExit = $LASTEXITCODE
487:     $text = (@($lines | ForEach-Object { [string]$_ }) -join "`n")
488:     if ($why) {
489:         Write-Output ("ERROR: the declared review mirror parent could not be read: " + $why)
490:         exit 2
491:     }
492:     if ($toolExit -ne 0) {
493:         Write-Output ("ERROR: the declared review mirror parent could not be read: artifact-roots.ps1 exited " + $toolExit + ": " + $text)
494:         exit 2
495:     }
496:     $row = ""
497:     try {
498:         $parsed = ConvertFrom-Json $text
499:         $row = [string]$parsed.reviewMirror
500:     } catch {
501:         $row = ""
502:     }
503:     if (-not $row) {
504:         Write-Output ("ERROR: the declared review mirror parent could not be read: no reviewMirror row in the tool's answer: " + $text)
505:         exit 2
506:     }
507:     $i = $row.IndexOf("<")
508:     if ($i -le 0) {
509:         Write-Output ("ERROR: the declared review mirror parent could not be read: the row has no per-debate placeholder (" + $row + ")")
510:         exit 2
511:     }
512:     $parent = $row.Substring(0, $i).Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/")
513:     if ((-not $parent) -or (-not [System.IO.Path]::IsPathRooted($parent))) {
514:         Write-Output ("ERROR: the declared review mirror parent could not be read: the row is not rooted (" + $row + ")")
515:         exit 2
516:     }
517:     return $parent
518: }
519: ```
520: 
521: (c) Change the signature to
522: ```powershell
523: function Resolve-ReapPath($label, $raw, $repoTop, $commonFull, $headFull, $allowRemediation, $mirrorParent) {
524: ```
525: extend its opening comment's rule list: after `commit the mirror tool makes over a tracked back-channel.` add ` Last of all, under the declared review mirror parent (any depth, never the parent itself), for the mirror and the bridge alike.`

527: (d) Replace the tail of the function, from `    if ($treeHead -eq $headFull) {` to the closing `exit 2` of the "not the attested head" message, with:
528: ```powershell
529:     $identityOk = ($treeHead -eq $headFull)
530:     if ((-not $identityOk) -and $allowRemediation) {
531:         $author = (& git --git-dir "$dotGit" log -1 --format=%ae HEAD 2>$null | Out-String).Trim()
532:         $authorExit = $LASTEXITCODE
533:         $parents = @(((& git --git-dir "$dotGit" rev-list --parents -n 1 HEAD 2>$null | Out-String).Trim()) -split "\s+")
534:         if (($authorExit -eq 0) -and ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0) -and ($author -eq "parallax@local") -and
535:             ($parents.Count -eq 2) -and ($parents[1] -eq $headFull)) {
536:             $identityOk = $true
537:         }
538:     }
539:     if (-not $identityOk) {
540:         Write-Output ("ERROR: $label is at $treeHead, not the attested head $headFull" +
541:             " - it is not the tree this verdict was issued on ($full)")
542:         exit 2
543:     }
544:     # LAST: the declared parent. StartsWith on the parent WITH its
545:     # separator, so a sibling whose name merely begins with the parent's
546:     # (C:/pxmx) is outside, and never Equals, so the parent itself is
547:     # never a tree. $p already carries the trailing separator.
548:     $parentSlash = $mirrorParent.TrimEnd("/") + "/"
549:     if ($p.Equals($parentSlash, $cmp) -or -not $p.StartsWith($parentSlash, $cmp)) {
550:         Write-Output ("ERROR: $label is not under the declared review mirror parent " + $mirrorParent + " ($full)")
551:         exit 2
552:     }
553:     return $full
554: ```
555: (`$p` and `$cmp` are the variables the overlap block above already defines.)

661: ## 10. Review mirror inventory

663: Observation only: this check reports and never deletes, whatever it

674: directory that cannot be measured is named as unmeasured and counted;

677: with its `ERROR:` line, never an empty inventory.

681:   NOTE with the three numbers.

683:   numbers and this fix: a finished debate's mirror is removed by the

7: # its own. This tool is the only reader. It prints the resolved set for
8: # the debate record, and -Assert answers whether one path lies inside a
9: # retained in-repo root before a retention copy runs.
10: #
11: # The declaration is PARSED here, never remembered: a missing or doubled
12: # line is an error, not a default.
13: #
14: # Windows PowerShell 5.1 and PowerShell 7, ASCII ONLY.
15: #
16: # Exit codes: 0 resolved (or -Assert inside the expected root, or inside
17: # any retained root when -Expect is absent), 1 -Assert outside every
18: # retained root or inside a retained root other than the one -Expect
19: # names, 2 parameter fault (anything wrong on the command line, a
20: # forbidden character in -DocsRoot or -Assert),
21: # unreadable declaration, or -RepoRoot not a git working tree. There is
22: # NO binding residual: this script has no param block, so PowerShell
23: # binds nothing, and every token reaches the parser below as a string
24: # on both hosts. The typed forms were measured 2026-09-13 by the diff
25: # debate: a missing value and a duplicate exited 1 from -File binding
26: # on both hosts, and `-Json:$true` exited 1 on 5.1 and 0 on 7 (switch
27: # conversion), so typed binding could not deliver one exit map. The map
28: # mirrors dispatch-round.ps1.

39: function Fail($message) {
40:     Write-Output ("ERROR: " + $message)
41:     exit 2

44: # ---- the command line --------------------------------------------------
45: # Hand-parsed from the RAW process command line, not $args: -File
46: # preprocessing drops an empty inline value (`-Assert:` as the last
47: # token vanishes, `-Json:` loses its colon) on both hosts before $args
48: # exists, so an intended assertion could answer 0 with no assertion at
49: # all (measured 2026-09-13 by the diff-debate R4 reviewer).
50: # [Environment]::GetCommandLineArgs() still carries every token on both
51: # hosts; the tokens after this script's own path are what is parsed.
52: # When the script is not the -File target (dot-sourced, or run from a
53: # host whose command line does not name it), $args is the fallback.
54: # Every fault is script-seen and exits 2 with an ERROR: line,
55: # identically on both hosts.
56: $RepoRoot = ""
57: $DocsRoot = ""
58: $Assert = ""
59: $Expect = ""
60: $Json = $false

62: $valueNames = @("RepoRoot", "DocsRoot", "Assert", "Expect")
63: $argv = @($args)
64: $rawArgs = @([Environment]::GetCommandLineArgs())
65: $self = $PSCommandPath
66: for ($ri = 0; $ri -lt $rawArgs.Count; $ri++) {
67:     $candidate = [string]$rawArgs[$ri]
68:     $same = $false
69:     try {

71:             [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($self), [System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)
72:     } catch { $same = $false }
73:     if ($same) {
74:         $argv = @()
75:         if (($ri + 1) -lt $rawArgs.Count) { $argv = @($rawArgs[($ri + 1)..($rawArgs.Count - 1)]) }
76:         break
77:     }
78: }
79: $ai = 0
80: while ($ai -lt $argv.Count) {
81:     $tok = [string]$argv[$ai]
82:     $m = [regex]::Match($tok, '^-([A-Za-z]+)(:(.*))?$')
83:     if (-not $m.Success) { Fail ("unknown parameter: " + $tok) }
84:     $given = $m.Groups[1].Value
85:     $name = ""
86:     foreach ($n in ($valueNames + @("Json"))) {
87:         if ($given -ieq $n) { $name = $n }
88:     }
89:     if (-not $name) { Fail ("unknown parameter: " + $tok) }
90:     if ($bound.ContainsKey($name)) { Fail ("-" + $name + " given more than once") }
91:     $bound[$name] = $true
92:     if ($name -eq "Json") {
93:         if ($m.Groups[2].Success) {
94:             $flag = $m.Groups[3].Value
95:             if (-not $flag) { Fail "-Json: has no value; write -Json, -Json:true or -Json:false" }
96:             if ($flag -match '^\$?true$') { $Json = $true }
97:             elseif ($flag -match '^\$?false$') { $Json = $false }
98:             else { Fail ("-Json takes true or false, not: " + $flag) }
99:         } else {

101:             # which PowerShell 7 evaluates to `True` and 5.1 leaves as
102:             # `$true`; both spellings select the format here.
103:             $Json = $true
104:             if (($ai + 1) -lt $argv.Count) {
105:                 $peek = [string]$argv[$ai + 1]
106:                 if ($peek -match '^\$?true$') { $Json = $true; $ai++ }
107:                 elseif ($peek -match '^\$?false$') { $Json = $false; $ai++ }
108:             }
109:         }
110:         $ai++
111:         continue
112:     }
113:     if ($m.Groups[2].Success) {
114:         $value = $m.Groups[3].Value
115:         if (-not $value) { Fail ("-" + $name + " is missing its value") }
116:     } else {
117:         $ai++
118:         if ($ai -ge $argv.Count) { Fail ("-" + $name + " is missing its value") }
119:         $value = [string]$argv[$ai]
120:         if ($value -match '^-[A-Za-z]') { Fail ("-" + $name + " is missing its value") }
121:     }

123:     $ai++
124: }

153: function Normalize-Slashes($p) {
154:     return $p.Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/")
155: }
156: 
157: function Resolve-Absolute($p) {
158:     # Provider-relative, like new-review-mirror.ps1: a relative path
159:     # resolves against PowerShell's location, not the process cwd. A
160:     # path the provider cannot resolve (an unknown drive, an illegal
161:     # character) is a parameter fault, exit 2, never an uncaught throw:
162:     # the failure is captured here and Fail is called OUTSIDE the try,
163:     # so nothing about `exit` inside a catch is relied on.
164:     $full = $null
165:     $why = ""
166:     try {
167:         $unresolved = $ExecutionContext.SessionState.Path.GetUnresolvedProviderPathFromPSPath($p)
168:         $full = Normalize-Slashes ([System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($unresolved))
169:     } catch {
170:         $why = $_.Exception.Message
171:     }
172:     if (-not $full) { Fail ("cannot resolve path '" + $p + "': " + $why) }
173:     return $full
174: }
175: 
176: # ---- the declaration ---------------------------------------------------
177: $NotesPath = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot "..\skills\multi-model-verify\references\model-prompting-notes.md"
178: if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $NotesPath -PathType Leaf)) {
179:     Fail ("declaration file not found: " + $NotesPath)
180: }
181: $notes = $null
182: $readWhy = ""
183: try {
184:     $notes = [System.IO.File]::ReadAllText($NotesPath, (New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)))
185: } catch {
186:     $readWhy = $_.Exception.Message
187: }
188: if ($null -eq $notes) { Fail ("declaration file unreadable: " + $NotesPath + ": " + $readWhy) }
189: $regionMatch = [regex]::Match($notes,
190:     '<!-- contract:start id=round-artifact-roots -->(.*?)<!-- contract:end -->',
191:     [System.Text.RegularExpressions.RegexOptions]::Singleline)
192: if (-not $regionMatch.Success) {
193:     Fail ("round-artifact-roots region not found in " + $NotesPath)
194: }
195: $region = $regionMatch.Groups[1].Value
196: 
197: $labels = @(
198:     @{ Key = "docsRoot";         Label = "Canonical docs root" },
199:     @{ Key = "docsRootOverride"; Label = "Canonical docs root override" },
200:     @{ Key = "frozenPlan";       Label = "Canonical frozen plan path" },
201:     @{ Key = "rounds";           Label = "Canonical rounds root" },
202:     @{ Key = "sddLedger";        Label = "Canonical SDD ledger root" },
203:     @{ Key = "reviewMirror";     Label = "Canonical review mirror root" },
204:     @{ Key = "attestation";      Label = "Canonical attestation root" },
205:     @{ Key = "checkpoint";       Label = "Canonical checkpoint root" }
206: )
207: $declared = @{}
208: foreach ($l in $labels) {
209:     $pattern = '(?m)^' + [regex]::Escape($l.Label) + ': `([^`\r\n]+)`[ \t]*\r?$'
210:     $hits = [regex]::Matches($region, $pattern)
211:     if ($hits.Count -ne 1) {
212:         Fail ("declaration line '" + $l.Label + "' found " + $hits.Count +
213:             " times in the round-artifact-roots region; expected exactly 1")
214:     }
215:     $declared[$l.Key] = $hits[0].Groups[1].Value
216: }
217: 
218: # ---- the repository ----------------------------------------------------
219: # Provider-relative FIRST. PowerShell starts a native child in its OWN
220: # current location, so `git -C .` itself runs in the right place; what
221: # breaks is the RELATIVE answer git prints (`.git`, `../.git`) reaching
222: # .NET GetFullPath, which resolves against the PROCESS working directory
223: # ([Environment]::CurrentDirectory) and not PowerShell's location.
224: # Resolving -RepoRoot here makes every later join absolute before any
225: # .NET path API sees it (measured 2026-09-12 by the R2 and R3 reviewers
226: # on both hosts; the same distinction new-review-mirror.ps1:1234 draws).
227: $RepoRoot = Resolve-Absolute $RepoRoot
228: if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $RepoRoot -PathType Container)) {
229:     Fail ("-RepoRoot is not a directory: " + $RepoRoot)
230: }
231: # Native git under Continue: a benign stderr line must not become a
232: # terminating error (CLAUDE.md, the dispatch traps).
233: $priorEap = $ErrorActionPreference
234: $ErrorActionPreference = "Continue"
235: $toplevel = (& git -C $RepoRoot rev-parse --show-toplevel 2>$null | Out-String).Trim()
236: $topExit = $LASTEXITCODE
237: $commonDir = (& git -C $RepoRoot rev-parse --git-common-dir 2>$null | Out-String).Trim()
238: $commonExit = $LASTEXITCODE
239: $ErrorActionPreference = $priorEap
240: if (($topExit -ne 0) -or -not $toplevel) {
241:     Fail ("-RepoRoot is not a git working tree: " + $RepoRoot)
242: }
243: if (($commonExit -ne 0) -or -not $commonDir) {
244:     Fail ("could not resolve the git common dir for " + $RepoRoot)
245: }
246: $top = Resolve-Absolute $toplevel
247: # A relative common dir is relative to the directory git RAN IN, which
248: # is -RepoRoot and not the toplevel: from a subdirectory git prints
249: # `../.git`, and joining that to the toplevel lands outside the checkout.
250: # Same join as tools/write-attestation.ps1.
251: if (-not [System.IO.Path]::IsPathRooted($commonDir)) {
252:     $commonDir = Join-Path $RepoRoot $commonDir
253: }
254: $common = Resolve-Absolute $commonDir
255: 
256: # ---- the docs root -----------------------------------------------------
257: if ($bound.ContainsKey("DocsRoot")) {
258:     $rel = $DocsRoot.Replace("\", "/").Trim("/")
259:     if (-not $rel) { Fail "-DocsRoot is empty" }
260:     # Validate BEFORE any path API: on Windows PowerShell 5.1 IsPathRooted
261:     # throws on `|`, on 7 it accepts and prints an unusable path
262:     # (measured 2026-09-12 by the R3 reviewer). One explicit set, both
263:     # hosts. The colon is rejected here as well as by the rooted check.
264:     if ($rel -match '[<>:"|?*\x00-\x1f]') {
265:         Fail ("-DocsRoot contains a character Windows paths forbid: " + $DocsRoot)
266:     }
267:     if ([System.IO.Path]::IsPathRooted($DocsRoot)) {
268:         Fail ("-DocsRoot must be relative to the repo root: " + $DocsRoot)
269:     }
270:     if (@($rel.Split("/")) -contains "..") {
271:         Fail ("-DocsRoot may not contain a '..' segment: " + $DocsRoot)
272:     }
273:     # Canonicalize through the filesystem rules, so `./other/root` and
274:     # `other//root` print as one spelling and -Assert compares equal.
275:     $docsFull = Resolve-Absolute (Join-Path $toplevel $rel)
276:     if ($docsFull.Equals($top, [System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) {
277:         Fail ("-DocsRoot may not be the repo root itself: " + $DocsRoot)
278:     }
279:     if (-not $docsFull.StartsWith($top + "/", [System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) {
280:         Fail ("-DocsRoot resolves outside the repo root: " + $DocsRoot)
281:     }
282:     $docsRel = $docsFull.Substring($top.Length + 1)
283:     $source = "-DocsRoot"
284: } elseif (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $toplevel $declared.docsRootOverride) -PathType Container) {
285:     $docsRel = $declared.docsRootOverride
286:     $source = "override directory exists"
287: } else {
288:     $docsRel = $declared.docsRoot
289:     $source = "default"
290: }
291: 
292: function Resolve-Row($value) {
293:     $v = $value.Replace("<docs-root>", $docsRel)
294:     $v = $v.Replace("<git-common-dir>", $common)
295:     # Split off the per-debate placeholder tail BEFORE any path API sees
296:     # the string: on Windows PowerShell 5.1, IsPathRooted and GetFullPath
297:     # throw "Illegal characters in path" on `<`, and on 7 they answer
298:     # False (measured 2026-09-12 by the R1 reviewer). The real parent is
299:     # resolved; the tail is appended verbatim.
300:     $i = $v.IndexOf("<")
301:     $tail = ""
302:     if ($i -ge 0) { $tail = $v.Substring($i); $v = $v.Substring(0, $i) }
303:     $v = $v.TrimEnd("/")
304:     if (-not $v) { Fail ("declaration row has no resolvable parent: " + $value) }
305:     if (-not [System.IO.Path]::IsPathRooted($v)) { $v = $top + "/" + $v }
306:     $parent = Resolve-Absolute $v
307:     if ($tail) { return $parent + "/" + $tail }
308:     return $parent
309: }
310: 
311: $resolved = [ordered]@{
312:     repo         = $top
313:     docsRoot     = $top + "/" + $docsRel
314:     source       = $source
315:     frozenPlan   = Resolve-Row $declared.frozenPlan
316:     rounds       = Resolve-Row $declared.rounds
317:     sddLedger    = Resolve-Row $declared.sddLedger
318:     reviewMirror = Resolve-Row $declared.reviewMirror
319:     attestation  = Resolve-Row $declared.attestation
320:     checkpoint   = Resolve-Row $declared.checkpoint
321: }
322: 
323: # ---- -Assert -----------------------------------------------------------
324: function Strip-Placeholder($p) {
325:     # The retained root is the path up to the first per-debate
326:     # placeholder: ".../plans/<date>-<topic>.md" asserts under ".../plans".
327:     $i = $p.IndexOf("<")
328:     $head = if ($i -ge 0) { $p.Substring(0, $i) } else { $p }
329:     return $head.TrimEnd("/")
330: }
331: 
332: $assertResult = $null
333: $exitCode = 0
334: if ($bound.ContainsKey("Assert")) {
335:     if (-not $Assert) { Fail "-Assert is empty" }
336:     # Same forbidden-character rule as -DocsRoot, minus the drive colon:
337:     # .NET Core's GetFullPath accepts `<`, `>` and `|`, so on PowerShell 7
338:     # an unsubstituted `<date>-<topic>` placeholder would resolve and could
339:     # answer inside (measured 2026-09-12 by the Task 2 review), while 5.1
340:     # throws. One explicit set, both hosts. Only a single drive-letter
341:     # prefix is exempted, so a provider-qualified form (FileSystem::C:\x)
342:     # that Resolve-Absolute could take is refused here; no caller passes
343:     # one, and the guard is deliberately stricter than the resolver.
344:     $assertBody = $Assert -replace '^[A-Za-z]:', ''
345:     if ($assertBody -match '[<>:"|?*\x00-\x1f]') {
346:         Fail ("-Assert contains a character Windows paths forbid: " + $Assert)
347:     }
348:     $target = Resolve-Absolute $Assert
349:     $cmp = [System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase
350:     # Rounds before the plan parent: the rounds root sits under it and
351:     # the more specific name is the useful answer. The review mirror
352:     # parent is last: it is outside the repository, and it is in the set
353:     # so that -Expect reviewMirror answers for a mirror path before the
354:     # build (backlog item 107) and a retention copy aimed at it is
355:     # refused by name. The SDD ledger row stays out: nothing copies
356:     # into it.
357:     $retained = @(
358:         @{ Name = "rounds root";         Root = Strip-Placeholder $resolved.rounds },
359:         @{ Name = "frozen plan parent";  Root = Strip-Placeholder $resolved.frozenPlan },
360:         @{ Name = "attestation root";    Root = Strip-Placeholder $resolved.attestation },
361:         @{ Name = "checkpoint root";     Root = Strip-Placeholder $resolved.checkpoint },
362:         @{ Name = "review mirror root";  Root = Strip-Placeholder $resolved.reviewMirror }
363:     )
364:     $inside = $null
365:     foreach ($r in $retained) {
366:         # The review mirror parent is the one root a path may never EQUAL:
367:         # a mirror is a tree under it, and the parent itself is never a
368:         # tree (the emitter refuses it too), so the answer for the
369:         # parent is outside, and the two readers agree.
370:         $mayEqual = ($r.Name -ne "review mirror root")
371:         if (($mayEqual -and $target.Equals($r.Root, $cmp)) -or $target.StartsWith($r.Root + "/", $cmp)) {
372:             $inside = $r.Name
373:             break
374:         }
375:     }
376:     if ($inside -and $expectedName -and ($inside -ne $expectedName)) {
377:         # Inside a retained root, but not the one the caller intends:
378:         # refused, and the answer names both roots.
379:         $assertResult = [ordered]@{ path = $target; inside = $false; root = $inside; expected = $expectedName }
380:         $exitCode = 1
381:     } elseif ($inside) {
382:         $assertResult = [ordered]@{ path = $target; inside = $true; root = $inside }
383:         if ($expectedName) { $assertResult["expected"] = $expectedName }
384:         $exitCode = 0
385:     } else {
386:         $assertResult = [ordered]@{ path = $target; inside = $false; root = "" }
387:         if ($expectedName) { $assertResult["expected"] = $expectedName }
388:         $exitCode = 1
389:     }
390: }
391: 
392: # ---- output ------------------------------------------------------------
393: if ($Json) {
394:     $out = [ordered]@{}
395:     foreach ($k in $resolved.Keys) { $out[$k] = $resolved[$k] }
396:     if ($assertResult) { $out["assert"] = $assertResult }
397:     Write-Output (ConvertTo-Json $out -Depth 3)
398: } else {
399:     Write-Output ("repo: " + $resolved.repo)
400:     Write-Output ("docs-root: " + $resolved.docsRoot)
401:     Write-Output ("docs-root source: " + $resolved.source)
402:     Write-Output ("frozen-plan: " + $resolved.frozenPlan)
403:     Write-Output ("rounds: " + $resolved.rounds)
404:     Write-Output ("sdd-ledger: " + $resolved.sddLedger)
405:     Write-Output ("review-mirror: " + $resolved.reviewMirror)
406:     Write-Output ("attestation: " + $resolved.attestation)
407:     Write-Output ("checkpoint: " + $resolved.checkpoint)
408:     if ($assertResult) {
409:         if ($assertResult.inside) {
410:             Write-Output ("assert: inside " + $assertResult.root + ": " + $assertResult.path)
411:         } elseif ($assertResult.root) {
412:             Write-Output ("assert: inside " + $assertResult.root + ", expected " +
413:                 $assertResult.expected + ": " + $assertResult.path)
414:         } else {
415:             Write-Output ("assert: outside every retained root: " + $assertResult.path)
416:         }
417:     }
418: }
419: exit $exitCode

1: # write-attestation.ps1 - record a multi-model-verify diff-mode TERMINAL
2: # verdict as a machine-readable, SHA-bound attestation.
3: #
4: # Written by the SESSION after its final adjudication - never from the
5: # reviewer's reply alone; the session's terminal verdict is the thing being
6: # recorded (family standing rule: a reviewer PASS/FIX is input, not
7: # terminal). Stored under the repo's GIT COMMON DIR, not the working tree:
8: # writing it cannot move HEAD out from under its own SHA, it never ships in
9: # a commit, and worktrees share it. verify-attestation.ps1 is the consumer
10: # (pre-push lanes, non-blocking v1).
11: #
12: # Windows PowerShell 5.1 compatible, ASCII ONLY (see check-drift.ps1
13: # header for why).
14: #
15: # Exit codes: 0 written, 2 argument/repo error, 3 written but a reap failed.
16: #
17: # REAP (0.36.0, backlog item 106): -ReapMirror and -ReapBridge name the
18: # review mirror and the clone bridge the debate ran on. The attestation
19: # is the one TERMINAL event the plugin records mechanically, so it is
20: # the reap point - never an age. Both paths are validated against the
21: # reviewed repository and the attested head BEFORE the record is
22: # written, so a refused argument costs nothing (exit 2); they are
23: # removed AFTER it, so a removal failure leaves the verdict standing
24: # and says so (exit 3). The removal itself is
25: # tools/review-tree-removal.ps1, shared with the mirror tool.
26: #
27: # PARENT (0.37.0, backlog item 107): both trees must also sit under the
28: # review mirror parent the round-artifact-roots declaration names,
29: # read here through tools/artifact-roots.ps1 rather than by a literal
30: # of this file's own, so that one edit to the row moves every reader.
31: # The rule is the LAST one in Resolve-ReapPath: every other refusal
32: # keeps its own message.

34:     [Parameter(Mandatory = $true)][string]$RepoRoot,

64: function Resolve-FullSha($repo, $sha, $label) {
65:     $full = (& git -C $repo rev-parse --verify --quiet ($sha + "^{commit}") 2>$null | Out-String).Trim()
66:     if (($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) -or -not $full) {
67:         Write-Output "ERROR: $label '$sha' does not resolve to a commit in $repo"
68:         exit 2
69:     }
70:     return $full
71: }

73: function Resolve-MirrorParent($repoRoot) {
74:     # The declared review mirror parent, read through the ONE reader of
75:     # the round-artifact-roots declaration, tools/artifact-roots.ps1,
76:     # invoked in-process so its exit reaches $LASTEXITCODE here (the
77:     # tool's documented $args fallback; measured 2026-09-13 on both
78:     # hosts). Every failure exits 2 with nothing written: a parent that
79:     # could not be read is never a parent that accepts everything.
80:     $tool = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot "artifact-roots.ps1"
81:     if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $tool -PathType Leaf)) {
82:         Write-Output "ERROR: the declared review mirror parent could not be read: $tool is missing"
83:         exit 2
84:     }
85:     $lines = @()
86:     $why = ""
87:     try {
88:         $lines = @(& $tool -RepoRoot $repoRoot -Json 2>&1)
89:     } catch {
90:         $why = $_.Exception.Message
91:     }
92:     $toolExit = $LASTEXITCODE
93:     $text = (@($lines | ForEach-Object { [string]$_ }) -join "`n")
94:     if ($why) {
95:         Write-Output ("ERROR: the declared review mirror parent could not be read: " + $why)
96:         exit 2
97:     }
98:     if ($toolExit -ne 0) {
99:         Write-Output ("ERROR: the declared review mirror parent could not be read: artifact-roots.ps1 exited " + $toolExit + ": " + $text)
100:         exit 2
101:     }
102:     $row = ""
103:     try {
104:         $parsed = ConvertFrom-Json $text
105:         $row = [string]$parsed.reviewMirror
106:     } catch {
107:         $row = ""
108:     }
109:     if (-not $row) {
110:         Write-Output ("ERROR: the declared review mirror parent could not be read: no reviewMirror row in the tool's answer: " + $text)
111:         exit 2
112:     }
113:     $i = $row.IndexOf("<")
114:     if ($i -le 0) {
115:         Write-Output ("ERROR: the declared review mirror parent could not be read: the row has no per-debate placeholder (" + $row + ")")
116:         exit 2
117:     }
118:     $parent = $row.Substring(0, $i).Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/")
119:     if ((-not $parent) -or (-not [System.IO.Path]::IsPathRooted($parent))) {
120:         Write-Output ("ERROR: the declared review mirror parent could not be read: the row is not rooted (" + $row + ")")
121:         exit 2
122:     }
123:     return $parent
124: }

126: function Resolve-ReapPath($label, $raw, $repoTop, $commonFull, $headFull, $allowRemediation, $mirrorParent) {
127:     # Returns the resolved full path of a tree this attestation may
128:     # remove, or prints ERROR and exits 2. Every refusal here runs before
129:     # the record is written. The rules are the spec's identity guard:
130:     # rooted and resolvable, not a filesystem root, an existing
131:     # directory, not through a link, not overlapping the reviewed repo
132:     # or its common dir, a .git DIRECTORY (a .git FILE is a linked
133:     # worktree, never a mirror or a bridge), and a HEAD equal to the
134:     # attested head - or, for the mirror only, one parallax@local
135:     # remediation commit whose single parent is that head, which is the
136:     # commit the mirror tool makes over a tracked back-channel. Last of
137:     # all, under the declared review mirror parent (any depth, never the
138:     # parent itself), for the mirror and the bridge alike.
139:     if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($raw)) {
140:         Write-Output "ERROR: $label is empty"
141:         exit 2
142:     }
143:     if (-not [System.IO.Path]::IsPathRooted($raw)) {
144:         Write-Output "ERROR: $label must be an absolute path ($raw)"
145:         exit 2
146:     }
147:     $full = $null
148:     try {
149:         $full = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($raw).TrimEnd("\")
150:     } catch {
151:         Write-Output ("ERROR: $label could not be resolved ($raw): " + $_.Exception.Message)
152:         exit 2
153:     }
154:     $pathRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($full)
155:     if ((-not $pathRoot) -or ($full.Length -le ([string]$pathRoot).TrimEnd("\").Length)) {
156:         Write-Output "ERROR: $label is a filesystem root ($full)"
157:         exit 2
158:     }
159:     $attr = 0
160:     try {
161:         $attr = [int][System.IO.File]::GetAttributes($full)
162:     } catch [System.IO.FileNotFoundException] {
163:         Write-Output "ERROR: $label does not exist ($full)"
164:         exit 2
165:     } catch [System.IO.DirectoryNotFoundException] {
166:         Write-Output "ERROR: $label does not exist ($full)"
167:         exit 2
168:     } catch {
169:         Write-Output ("ERROR: $label could not be examined ($full): " + $_.Exception.Message)
170:         exit 2
171:     }
172:     if (($attr -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::Directory) -eq 0) {
173:         Write-Output "ERROR: $label is a file, not a tree ($full)"
174:         exit 2
175:     }
176:     $hit = Test-PathOrAncestorIsLink $full
177:     if ($hit) {
178:         Write-Output ("ERROR: $label passes through a directory link at $hit" +
179:             " - a tree is never removed through a link")
180:         exit 2
181:     }
182:     # The mirror tool's own overlap comparison, against the reviewed
183:     # repository and against its git common dir (a linked worktree's
184:     # common dir sits outside its top level).
185:     $cmp = [System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase
186:     $p = $full.Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/") + "/"
187:     foreach ($pair in @(@("the reviewed repository", $repoTop), @("the git common dir", $commonFull))) {
188:         $g = ([string]$pair[1]).Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/") + "/"
189:         if ($p.Equals($g, $cmp)) {
190:             Write-Output ("ERROR: $label is " + $pair[0] + " itself ($full)")
191:             exit 2
192:         }
193:         if ($p.StartsWith($g, $cmp)) {
194:             Write-Output ("ERROR: $label is inside " + $pair[0] + " ($full)")
195:             exit 2
196:         }
197:         if ($g.StartsWith($p, $cmp)) {
198:             Write-Output ("ERROR: $label contains " + $pair[0] + " ($full)")
199:             exit 2
200:         }
201:     }
202:     $dotGit = Join-Path $full ".git"
203:     $ga = 0
204:     try {
205:         $ga = [int][System.IO.File]::GetAttributes($dotGit)
206:     } catch {
207:         Write-Output "ERROR: $label carries no .git entry, so it is not a review tree ($full)"
208:         exit 2
209:     }
210:     if (($ga -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::Directory) -eq 0) {
211:         Write-Output ("ERROR: $label has a .git FILE, which marks a linked worktree" +
212:             " - never a mirror or a bridge ($full)")
213:         exit 2
214:     }
215:     if (($ga -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint) -ne 0) {
216:         Write-Output ("ERROR: $label has a .git that is a directory link, so its" +
217:             " identity would be read through the link ($full)")
218:         exit 2
219:     }
220:     $treeHead = (& git --git-dir "$dotGit" rev-parse --verify --quiet "HEAD^{commit}" 2>$null | Out-String).Trim()
221:     if (($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) -or -not $treeHead) {
222:         Write-Output "ERROR: $label has no readable HEAD ($full)"
223:         exit 2
224:     }
225:     $identityOk = ($treeHead -eq $headFull)
226:     if ((-not $identityOk) -and $allowRemediation) {
227:         $author = (& git --git-dir "$dotGit" log -1 --format=%ae HEAD 2>$null | Out-String).Trim()
228:         $authorExit = $LASTEXITCODE
229:         $parents = @(((& git --git-dir "$dotGit" rev-list --parents -n 1 HEAD 2>$null | Out-String).Trim()) -split "\s+")
230:         if (($authorExit -eq 0) -and ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0) -and ($author -eq "parallax@local") -and
231:             ($parents.Count -eq 2) -and ($parents[1] -eq $headFull)) {
232:             $identityOk = $true
233:         }
234:     }
235:     if (-not $identityOk) {
236:         Write-Output ("ERROR: $label is at $treeHead, not the attested head $headFull" +
237:             " - it is not the tree this verdict was issued on ($full)")
238:         exit 2
239:     }
240:     # LAST: the declared parent. StartsWith on the parent WITH its
241:     # separator, so a sibling whose name merely begins with the parent's
242:     # (C:/pxmx) is outside, and never Equals, so the parent itself is
243:     # never a tree. $p already carries the trailing separator.
244:     $parentSlash = $mirrorParent.TrimEnd("/") + "/"
245:     if ($p.Equals($parentSlash, $cmp) -or -not $p.StartsWith($parentSlash, $cmp)) {
246:         Write-Output ("ERROR: $label is not under the declared review mirror parent " + $mirrorParent + " ($full)")
247:         exit 2
248:     }
249:     return $full
250: }

252: function Invoke-Reap($label, $full, $notAttempted) {
253:     # Runs after the record is written: a failure here is exit 3, with
254:     # the attestation left standing and the reason named. $notAttempted
255:     # names any other reap tree that was skipped as a result, so the
256:     # failure message tells the caller everything left to remove by hand.
257:     $r = Remove-ReviewTree $full
258:     if (-not $r.Ok) {
259:         Write-Output ("ERROR: reap failed for " + $full + ": " + $r.Reason +
260:             " - the attestation stands; remove the " + $label + " by hand" + $notAttempted)
261:         exit 3
262:     }
263:     Write-Output ("reaped " + $label + ": " + $full)
264: }
265: 
266: $toplevel = (& git -C $RepoRoot rev-parse --show-toplevel 2>$null | Out-String).Trim()
267: if (($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) -or -not $toplevel) {
268:     Write-Output "ERROR: $RepoRoot is not a git repository"
269:     exit 2
270: }
271: # Common dir, not git-dir: a review adjudicated inside a worktree must
272: # attest the repo every other worktree (and the pre-push hook) sees.
273: $commonDir = (& git -C $RepoRoot rev-parse --git-common-dir 2>$null | Out-String).Trim()
274: if (-not $commonDir) {
275:     Write-Output "ERROR: could not resolve the git common dir for $RepoRoot"
276:     exit 2
277: }
278: if (-not [System.IO.Path]::IsPathRooted($commonDir)) {
279:     $commonDir = Join-Path $RepoRoot $commonDir
280: }
281: 
282: $baseFull = Resolve-FullSha $RepoRoot $BaseSha "BaseSha"
283: $headFull = Resolve-FullSha $RepoRoot $HeadSha "HeadSha"
284: if ($baseFull -eq $headFull) {
285:     Write-Output "ERROR: BaseSha and HeadSha resolve to the same commit - nothing was reviewed"
286:     exit 2
287: }
288: 
289: # REAP VALIDATION, before anything is written. Both trees are resolved
290: # and checked here so a wrong argument is refused at exit 2 with the
291: # record unwritten, and the removal below runs only against paths this
292: # block accepted.
293: $commonFull = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($commonDir).TrimEnd("\")
294: $reapMirrorFull = $null
295: $reapBridgeFull = $null
296: $mirrorParent = $null
297: if ($ReapMirror -or $ReapBridge) {
298:     # Read once, and only when a tree is named: an emitter run without a
299:     # reap parameter never touches the declaration.
300:     $mirrorParent = Resolve-MirrorParent $RepoRoot
301: }
302: if ($ReapMirror) {
303:     $reapMirrorFull = Resolve-ReapPath "the reap mirror" $ReapMirror $toplevel $commonFull $headFull $true $mirrorParent
304: }
305: if ($ReapBridge) {
306:     $reapBridgeFull = Resolve-ReapPath "the reap bridge" $ReapBridge $toplevel $commonFull $headFull $false $mirrorParent
307: }
308: if ($reapMirrorFull -and $reapBridgeFull) {
309:     $cmp = [System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase
310:     $m = $reapMirrorFull.Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/") + "/"
311:     $b = $reapBridgeFull.Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/") + "/"
312:     if ($m.Equals($b, $cmp) -or $m.StartsWith($b, $cmp) -or $b.StartsWith($m, $cmp)) {
313:         Write-Output ("ERROR: the reap mirror and the reap bridge overlap (" + $reapMirrorFull + ", " + $reapBridgeFull + ") - name two separate trees")
314:         exit 2
315:     }
316: }
317: 
318: $attDir = Join-Path (Join-Path $commonDir "parallax") "attestations"
319: New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $attDir | Out-Null
320: 
321: # Schema 2 (0.7.0): checkpoint_binding is the emitter-authored
322: # DECLARATION of whether this record carries checkpoint metadata -
323: # without it, deleting the binding fields would downgrade a bound record
324: # to legacy-unbound and the verifier could not tell (Sol round 2).
325: $att = [ordered]@{
326:     schema              = 2
327:     repo                = (Split-Path $toplevel -Leaf)
328:     mode                = $Mode
329:     base_sha            = $baseFull
330:     head_sha            = $headFull
331:     verdict             = $Verdict
332:     verification_status = $VerificationStatus
333:     rounds              = $Rounds
334:     participants        = $Participants
335:     route_note          = $RouteNote
336:     checkpoint_binding  = "none"
337:     stamp               = (Get-Date -Format "yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss")
338: }
339: if ($CheckpointFile) {
340:     if (-not (Test-Path $CheckpointFile)) {
341:         Write-Output "ERROR: checkpoint file not found: $CheckpointFile"
342:         exit 2
343:     }
344:     $cpFull = (Resolve-Path $CheckpointFile).Path
345:     $cpDir = Join-Path (Join-Path $commonDir "parallax") "application-checkpoints"
346:     $cpDirFull = if (Test-Path $cpDir) { (Resolve-Path $cpDir).Path } else { $null }
347:     if ((-not $cpDirFull) -or ((Split-Path $cpFull -Parent) -ne $cpDirFull)) {
348:         Write-Output "ERROR: checkpoint must live under $cpDir - the verifier re-hashes it there"
349:         exit 2
350:     }
351:     $sha = [System.Security.Cryptography.SHA256]::Create()
352:     $bytes = [System.IO.File]::ReadAllBytes($cpFull)
353:     $cpHash = ([System.BitConverter]::ToString($sha.ComputeHash($bytes)) -replace '-', '').ToLower()
354:     $changed = @(& git -C $RepoRoot diff --name-only ($baseFull + ".." + $headFull) 2>$null | Where-Object { $_ })
355:     if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
356:         Write-Output "ERROR: could not compute the changed-path set for $baseFull..$headFull"
357:         exit 2
358:     }
359:     $att["checkpoint_binding"] = "bound"
360:     $att["checkpoint_file"] = (Split-Path $CheckpointFile -Leaf)
361:     $att["checkpoint_hash"] = $cpHash
362:     $att["changed_paths"] = $changed
363: }
364: $outFile = Join-Path $attDir ($headFull + ".json")
365: $json = $att | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 3
366: try {
367:     Set-Content -LiteralPath $outFile -Value $json -Encoding ASCII -NoNewline -ErrorAction Stop
368: } catch {
369:     Write-Output ("ERROR: the attestation could not be written to " + $outFile + ": " + $_.Exception.Message + " - nothing was reaped")
370:     exit 2
371: }
372: $writtenText = $null
373: try {
374:     $writtenText = [System.IO.File]::ReadAllText($outFile)
375: } catch {
376:     $writtenText = $null
377: }
378: # Ordinal: -ne is case-insensitive on strings, so it would accept a read-back that differs only in letter case.
379: if (-not [string]::Equals($writtenText, $json, [System.StringComparison]::Ordinal)) {
380:     Write-Output ("ERROR: the attestation on disk does not match what was written (" + $outFile + ") - nothing was reaped")
381:     exit 2
382: }
383: Write-Output "attestation written: $outFile ($Verdict, $baseFull..$headFull)"
384: # THE REAP, after the record and in this order: mirror, its advisory
385: # sidecar, then the bridge. A failure stops at the first tree that
386: # could not be removed (exit 3) and leaves the record standing.
387: if ($reapMirrorFull) {
388:     $bridgeNote = ""
389:     if ($reapBridgeFull) { $bridgeNote = "; the bridge was not attempted: " + $reapBridgeFull }
390:     Invoke-Reap "mirror" $reapMirrorFull $bridgeNote
391:     # The mirror tool's advisory sibling, `<mirror>.source-manifest`,
392:     # removed only when it is an ordinary file: a directory or a link
393:     # there is not the sidecar and is left alone.
394:     $sidecar = $reapMirrorFull + ".source-manifest"
395:     $sa = $null
396:     try {
397:         $sa = [int][System.IO.File]::GetAttributes($sidecar)
398:     } catch [System.IO.FileNotFoundException] {
399:         $sa = $null
400:     } catch [System.IO.DirectoryNotFoundException] {
401:         $sa = $null
402:     } catch {
403:         Write-Output ("ERROR: reap failed for " + $sidecar + ": the sidecar could not be examined: " +
404:             $_.Exception.Message + " - the attestation stands; remove the sidecar by hand" + $bridgeNote)
405:         exit 3
406:     }
407:     if (($null -ne $sa) -and
408:         (($sa -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::Directory) -eq 0) -and
409:         (($sa -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint) -eq 0)) {
410:         try {
411:             [System.IO.File]::SetAttributes($sidecar, [System.IO.FileAttributes]::Normal)
412:             [System.IO.File]::Delete($sidecar)
413:         } catch {
414:             Write-Output ("ERROR: reap failed for " + $sidecar + ": " + $_.Exception.Message +
415:                 " - the attestation stands; remove the sidecar by hand" + $bridgeNote)
416:             exit 3
417:         }
418:         # THE POSTCONDITION, read back rather than inferred from the
419:         # absence of an exception (tools/review-tree-removal.ps1:167-179
420:         # does the same for the tree root).
421:         try {
422:             [void][System.IO.File]::GetAttributes($sidecar)
423:             Write-Output ("ERROR: reap failed for " + $sidecar + ": the sidecar still exists after removal" +
424:                 " - the attestation stands; remove the sidecar by hand" + $bridgeNote)
425:             exit 3
426:         } catch [System.IO.FileNotFoundException] {
427:             # gone: fall through to the success line below
428:         } catch [System.IO.DirectoryNotFoundException] {
429:             # gone: fall through to the success line below
430:         } catch {
431:             Write-Output ("ERROR: reap failed for " + $sidecar + ": the sidecar could not be re-examined after removal: " +
432:                 $_.Exception.Message + " - the attestation stands; remove the sidecar by hand" + $bridgeNote)
433:             exit 3
434:         }
435:         Write-Output ("reaped sidecar: " + $sidecar)
436:     }
437: }
438: if ($reapBridgeFull) {
439:     Invoke-Reap "bridge" $reapBridgeFull ""
440: }
441: exit 0

61: def make_repo(tmp_path, name="repo"):
62:     """A repo with a base commit and a feature commit, returned with both
63:     SHAs, because an attestation needs a real base..head range."""
64:     repo = tmp_path / name
65:     repo.mkdir()
66:     subprocess.run(["git", "init", "-q", "-b", "main"], cwd=repo,
67:                    check=True, capture_output=True)
68:     (repo / "a.txt").write_text("a\n", encoding="utf-8")
69:     git(repo, "add", "a.txt")
70:     git(repo, "commit", "-q", "-m", "base")
71:     base = git(repo, "rev-parse", "HEAD")
72:     git(repo, "checkout", "-q", "-b", "feat")
73:     (repo / "b.txt").write_text("b\n", encoding="utf-8")
74:     git(repo, "add", "b.txt")
75:     git(repo, "commit", "-q", "-m", "feat")
76:     head = git(repo, "rev-parse", "HEAD")
77:     return repo, base, head

80: def make_mirror(repo, path):
81:     """A mirror the way the mirror tool makes one: a FILE COPY that keeps
82:     .git, so it carries git's read-only object files."""
83:     shutil.copytree(repo, path)
84:     return path
85: 
86: 
87: def make_bridge(repo, path, branch="feat"):
88:     """A clone bridge the way a KitnEssentials session makes one, checked
89:     out after a --no-checkout clone."""
90:     subprocess.run(["git", "clone", "-q", "--no-checkout", str(repo), str(path)],
91:                    check=True, capture_output=True)
92:     git(path, "checkout", "-q", branch)
93:     return path
94: 
95: 
96: def junction(link, target):
97:     subprocess.run(["cmd", "/c", "mklink", "/J", str(link), str(target)],
98:                    check=True, capture_output=True)

103:     return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

114: def _clear_readonly_and_retry(func, path, exc):
115:     os.chmod(path, stat.S_IWRITE)
116:     func(path)

121:     """A fresh `C:\\pxm\\t-<8 hex>` for one test, removed on the way out
122:     whatever the test left behind (a held-handle case leaves its trees).
123:     Git's read-only object files are made writable as they are hit.
124:     Nothing else under the parent is ever touched."""
125:     d = PARENT / ("t-" + uuid.uuid4().hex[:8])
126:     d.mkdir(parents=True)
127:     yield d
128:     if d.exists():
129:         shutil.rmtree(d, onexc=_clear_readonly_and_retry)

 3. **The rule covers `-ReapBridge`.** The bridge is built beside the mirror (`C:\pxm\kvs-<tag>`), the doctor counts it in the same inventory, and a bridge left outside the parent is exactly a tree the reaper should not touch by name.
 4. **The emitter reads the parent through `tools/artifact-roots.ps1`, never by its own literal.** Item 100 exists because each writer read the location on its own. The resolver runs in-process (`& <tool> -RepoRoot <repo> -Json`), which the tool supports through its documented `$args` fallback, so `exit 2` inside it reaches `$LASTEXITCODE` in the emitter (measured 2026-09-13 on both hosts); any failure to read the parent exits 2 with nothing written. The read happens only when a reap parameter is given, so an emitter run without `-ReapMirror`/`-ReapBridge` never touches the resolver.
 5. **The guard is the LAST rule in `Resolve-ReapPath`.** Every existing refusal fires first with its own message, so the existing refusal tests keep their trees in `tmp_path` and still name their reason; the parent guard is proven by trees that pass every earlier rule.
-6. **The mirror tool does not enforce the parent.** `tools/new-review-mirror.ps1` takes `-MirrorPath` and does not read the declaration; its callers include every test fixture that builds a mirror in a temporary directory. The check before a build is `tools/artifact-roots.ps1 -Assert <mirror-path> -Expect reviewMirror`, run by the session; the check that matters is the emitter's, because the removal is the dangerous act. A mirror built elsewhere is refused at reap and removed by hand, which the prose says.

13: function Test-PathOrAncestorIsLink($path) {
14:     # The path itself, then each existing ancestor up to the drive root:
15:     # is any of them a reparse point? Attributes are read directly
16:     # rather than after a Test-Path, because a DANGLING junction is a
17:     # reparse point Test-Path may report as absent; a missing entry is
18:     # the one condition that skips a level, and it is recognised by the
19:     # exception type, never by a false from a helper. Returns the
20:     # offending path or $null.
21:     $probe = ([string]$path).TrimEnd("\", "/")
22:     while ($probe -and -not [string]::IsNullOrEmpty([System.IO.Path]::GetFileName($probe))) {
23:         $pa = 0
24:         $missing = $false
25:         try {
26:             $pa = [int][System.IO.File]::GetAttributes($probe)
27:         } catch [System.IO.FileNotFoundException] {
28:             $missing = $true
29:         } catch [System.IO.DirectoryNotFoundException] {
30:             $missing = $true
31:         } catch {
32:             Write-Output ("ERROR: " + $probe + " could not be read while" +
33:                 " checking for a directory link: " + $_.Exception.Message)
34:             exit 2
35:         }
36:         if (-not $missing -and (($pa -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint) -ne 0)) {
37:             return $probe
38:         }
39:         $probe = [System.IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($probe)
40:     }
41:     return $null
42: }
43: 
44: function Remove-ReviewTreeEntries($dir, $depth) {
45:     # Empties $dir in post-order. Returns $null when every entry beneath
46:     # it is gone, else the reason, which names the entry that stopped it.
47:     #
48:     # NEVER THROUGH A LINK. A directory link is removed with the
49:     # non-recursive Directory.Delete and a file link with File.Delete;
50:     # both remove the link and never its target. Measured 2026-09-13 on
51:     # Windows PowerShell 5.1 and PowerShell 7, intact and dangling. An
52:     # ordinary directory is emptied by this walk and then removed with
53:     # the same non-recursive Directory.Delete, which throws when
54:     # anything survived, so a partial removal cannot read as a whole
55:     # one. An ordinary file has its read-only bit cleared first, because
56:     # git marks its object files read-only and File.Delete refuses them
57:     # as they are (measured the same day).
58:     if ($depth -gt 128) {
59:         return ($dir + " sits deeper than 128 levels; the walk stops" +
60:             " rather than assume the tree ends")
61:     }
62:     $entries = $null
63:     try {
64:         $entries = [System.IO.Directory]::GetFileSystemEntries($dir)
65:     } catch {
66:         return ($dir + " could not be listed: " + $_.Exception.Message)
67:     }
68:     foreach ($entry in $entries) {
69:         $ea = 0
70:         try {
71:             $ea = [int][System.IO.File]::GetAttributes($entry)
72:         } catch {
73:             return ($entry + " could not be examined: " + $_.Exception.Message)
74:         }
75:         $isDir = (($ea -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::Directory) -ne 0)
76:         $isLink = (($ea -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint) -ne 0)
77:         try {
78:             if ($isLink) {
79:                 if ($isDir) {
80:                     [System.IO.Directory]::Delete($entry)
81:                 } else {
82:                     [System.IO.File]::Delete($entry)
83:                 }
84:             } elseif ($isDir) {
85:                 $inner = Remove-ReviewTreeEntries $entry ($depth + 1)
86:                 if ($null -ne $inner) { return $inner }
87:                 # robocopy /DCOPY:DA carries a read-only directory into the mirror; Directory.Delete refuses it as it is (measured 2026-09-13, both hosts).
88:                 if (($ea -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::ReadOnly) -ne 0) {
89:                     [System.IO.File]::SetAttributes($entry, [System.IO.FileAttributes]::Directory)
90:                 }
91:                 [System.IO.Directory]::Delete($entry)
92:             } else {
93:                 if (($ea -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::ReadOnly) -ne 0) {
94:                     [System.IO.File]::SetAttributes($entry, [System.IO.FileAttributes]::Normal)
95:                 }
