Seat: parallax:fable-reviewer (same-harness, read-only; agent a1b90caa763edcb37). Brief: brief-fable-diff-r1.md. Raw reply verbatim below.

---

### Strengths

- The parent guard is genuinely last and fail-closed. `tools/write-attestation.ps1:233-260` (package lines 1533-1561) replaces the two early `return $full` paths with an `$identityOk` flag so the head rule, the remediation-commit rule and the new parent rule run in one straight line; `test_the_parent_itself_is_never_a_reap_path` (`evals/multi-model-verify/test_mirror_reaper.py`, package lines 883-892) pins both the ordering and the exact `StartsWith`/`Equals` form.
- The parent read cannot degrade into an accepting parent. `Resolve-MirrorParent` (`tools/write-attestation.ps1:62-124`) exits 2 on a missing tool, a thrown invocation, a non-zero tool exit, unparsable JSON, a missing `reviewMirror` key, a row with no `<` placeholder, and an unrooted parent. The tool it calls ends with an explicit `exit $exitCode` (`tools/artifact-roots.ps1:414`), so `$LASTEXITCODE` is never stale. Both failure directions have live tests: `test_an_unreadable_parent_refuses_every_reap` and `test_a_roots_tool_that_exits_nonzero_refuses_every_reap` (package lines 895-963), the second added from a ledger minor rather than deferred.
- The in-process `$args` fallback is sound. `tools/artifact-roots.ps1:63-78` scans `GetCommandLineArgs()` for its own `$PSCommandPath`; when the emitter is the `-File` target that scan never matches and `$argv = @($args)` carries the emitter's `-RepoRoot <repo> -Json`. The success cases under `pxm` prove it on both hosts.
- The guard's input is already canonical and link-checked. `$p` is built from `GetFullPath($raw)` (`tools/write-attestation.ps1:149,186`), so `..` traversal and separator variants normalize away, and `Test-PathOrAncestorIsLink` (`tools/review-tree-removal.ps1:13-22`) walks every ancestor, so a junction or mount point anywhere under `C:\pxm` is refused before the parent rule is reached.
- The retired form cannot come back silently: the `<TEMP>` sweep shape plus its can-fail proof (`test_artifact_roots.py`, package lines 399-400, 426-433), and the parent-spelling binder with `[/\\]+` and the `.` lookahead (package lines 448-488) tie every prose example to the declaration row.
- The `pxm` fixture is contained: a fresh `C:\pxm\t-<8 hex>` per test, `rmtree` with `onexc` clearing git's read-only bits, and every tree a success case names sits inside it (package lines 553-563 and the relocated cases). CI runs Python 3.12 (`.github/workflows/skill-evals.yml:27,99`), so `onexc` is available there.
- Constraints held: `SKILL.md` carries exactly the one quoted line edit (package lines 1033-1034); `never inside the reviewed repository` is whole on one physical line (`model-prompting-notes.md:808`); the eight declaration lines are unchanged in count; all three commit messages are flag-free.

### Issues

#### Critical

None.

#### Important

- `skills/multi-model-verify/references/model-prompting-notes.md:822` tells the session to run `tools/artifact-roots.ps1 -Assert <mirror-path> -Expect reviewMirror`. The tool refuses that command line: `tools/artifact-roots.ps1:125` fails with `-RepoRoot is required` (exit 2) before any assertion runs. The correct form is the one `references/preflight-mirror.md:22` carries, `-RepoRoot <repo> -Assert <scratch> -Expect reviewMirror`. This is the declaration's own file describing its own check, and the pin `"-Expect reviewMirror" in tail` (package line 235) accepts either spelling, so nothing catches it. The failure direction is closed (a session copying it gets exit 2, not a false clean), which is why this is Important rather than Critical. It is a plan defect: Task 1 Step 2 dictates this text verbatim (plan lines 227-228).

#### Minor

- The pre-build check and the reap guard disagree on the parent itself. `tools/artifact-roots.ps1:379` (package line 1390) accepts `Equals($r.Root)`, so `-Assert C:\pxm -Expect reviewMirror` exits 0, while the emitter refuses that path (`tools/write-attestation.ps1:257`). Both prose sites say exit 0 "is the only clean answer" (`preflight-mirror.md:23`, `model-prompting-notes.md:822-824`), so a session that passes the parent as `<scratch>` gets a clean pre-build answer for a tree that can never be reaped, and `new-review-mirror.ps1 -MirrorPath C:\pxm -Force` would rebuild over every sibling. Plan decision 1's interface states the assert rule deliberately, so this is a plan-level choice worth one sentence in the prose ("a direct child, never the parent"), not a code fix.
- `test_mirror_reaper.py` Group 7 duplicates the fake-plugin setup verbatim in two tests (package lines 902-913 and 941-951). A `doctored_plugin(tmp_path, replacement)` helper would keep the third failure direction, if one is ever added, from copying it a third time. Polish only.
- Ledger record: the Task 2 note "Equals branch unreachable behind the .git rule" is wrong in its reasoning, not its conclusion. `C:\pxm` can itself carry a `.git` directory at the attested head (a session that passed `-MirrorPath C:\pxm`), and then the `Equals` branch is the rule that fires. The code is right to keep it; the ledger line should not be cited as a reason to remove it later.

Answers to the five questions, with the evidence above:

1. Readers still pointing at temp or the drive root: none. The surface sweep for `temp directory`, `$env:TEMP`, `GetTempPath`, `<TEMP>`, `drive root`, `kv-<tag>` and `C:\kv-` across `skills/`, `agents/`, `commands/`, `tools/` and `hooks/` finds only the doctor's legacy line (`commands/doctor.md:413-417`, labelled as such), historical prose in the notes bullet, and three unrelated `$env:TEMP` uses (`commands/doctor.md:68-69,243,347`, `tools/check-drift.ps1:817`, `tools/new-kimi-lane-home.ps1:182`) that belong to the lane login and the drift worktree, not mirrors.
2. A tree outside `C:\pxm` through an accepted spelling: not found. `GetFullPath` canonicalizes `..` and slashes; `C:/pxmx/` fails the separator-suffixed `StartsWith`; `\\?\C:\pxm\...` and `\\localhost\C$\pxm\...` are refused (they do not start with `C:/pxm/`), which is the safe direction; a subst drive or a junction/mount point under the parent is refused by the ancestor link walk. Case variants are the same NTFS directory.
3. A failed parent read reading as a parent: no path found; every branch of `Resolve-MirrorParent` exits 2 before `Resolve-ReapPath` is called, and `$mirrorParent` is only ever the function's return value.
4. Tests under the real `C:\pxm`: nothing outside the per-test `t-*` directory is touched; the held-handle and held-sidecar cases close their handles before teardown; the junction target lives in `tmp_path`. Stated limit: if `rmtree` fails even after the chmod retry, the `t-*` directory is orphaned and no later sweep removes it; the doctor would count it. `C:\pxm` itself is created on a fresh runner and never removed (ledger, no action).
5. Executable as written: `preflight-mirror.md:22` and doctor check 10 are; `model-prompting-notes.md:822` is not (the Important above).

Not verified by me, named as gaps: `skill_lint` token count after the SKILL.md edit, the item 107 digest, ASCII/LF on the two tools, and both-host pytest runs. The ledger and the session's gate are the evidence for those.

- T3: frozen plan Task 3 doctor text carries the unprefixed citation: fix-before-merge as a dated in-place correction, in the same fix wave, so the plan and the shipped doctor agree.

552: # A dated citation under the declared rounds root
553: # (docs/superpowers/plans/rounds/<date>-...) matches none of them; a
554: # dated ledger citation (.superpowers/sdd/<date>-...) is exempted by the
555: # lookahead, because one exists in the notes today. Every separator is
556: # `[/\\]` because tools/*.ps1 spell paths with backslashes. The
557: # lookbehind in the first shape only keeps a citation of a nested
558: # `plans/rounds/` path from being misread as a root beside plans/; the
559: # string `plans/superpowers/rounds/` does not occur in the tree.
560: # STATED LIMITS, the forms these shapes do not catch: a slashless
561: # spelling (`superpowers rounds`, `dev docs superpowers`), a ledger root
562: # with no separator after `sdd`, a root assembled from parts at runtime
563: # (`Join-Path $common "parallax"`), and any spelling on a surface the
564: # glob list above does not name. The writer test in Group 3b is what
565: # binds the runtime-assembled rows.

595:                 if f == NOTES and lineno in exempt:
596:                     continue

600:                             f"{f.relative_to(REPO).as_posix()}:{lineno}: {label}")
601:     searched = "; ".join(label for label, _ in FORBIDDEN_SHAPES)
602:     assert not offenders, (
603:         "a round root is named outside the declaration (searched for: "
604:         + searched + "):\n" + "\n".join(offenders))

688: PARENT_SPELLING = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z]:[/\\]+pxm(?![A-Za-z0-9_.-])", re.I)

691: def declared_mirror_parent():
692:     """The parent the declaration names, normalized: `C:/pxm/<short-name>/`
693:     gives `c:/pxm`."""
694:     m = MIRROR_ROW.search(read(NOTES))
695:     assert m, "the review mirror row is not in the declared shape"
696:     return norm(m.group(1))

699: def test_every_parent_spelling_on_the_surface_is_the_declared_one():
700:     # Prose and the doctor NAME the parent as an example (`C:\pxm\kv-<tag>`).
701:     # An example is not a second declaration only while it agrees with
702:     # the row: every drive-rooted `pxm` spelling on the plugin surface is
703:     # the declared parent, so a renamed row turns each stale example red.
704:     parent = declared_mirror_parent()
705:     assert parent == "c:/pxm", parent
706:     seen = 0
707:     for pattern in PLUGIN_SURFACE:
708:         for f in sorted(REPO.glob(pattern)):
709:             if not f.is_file():
710:                 continue
711:             for lineno, line in enumerate(read(f).splitlines(), 1):
712:                 for m in PARENT_SPELLING.finditer(line):
713:                     seen += 1
714:                     assert re.sub(r"[/\\]+", "/", m.group(0)).lower() == parent, (
715:                         f"{f.relative_to(REPO).as_posix()}:{lineno} names "
716:                         f"{m.group(0)}, not the declared parent")
717:     assert seen >= 4, "the notes, the prose examples and the doctor should name the parent"

629:     row = "Canonical review mirror root: `C:/pxm/<short-name>/`\n"

631:     (notes_dir / "model-prompting-notes.md").write_text(
632:         read(notes).replace(row, replacement), encoding="utf-8")
633:     return fake / "tools" / "write-attestation.ps1"

639: def test_a_tree_outside_the_declared_parent_is_refused_for_mirror_and_bridge(tmp_path):
640:     # The LAST rule of the identity guard: a mirror and a bridge that
641:     # pass every other rule, built under the host temp directory as every
642:     # tree was until 2026-09-13, are refused by name with the record
643:     # unwritten. The message names the declared parent.
644:     repo, base, head = make_repo(tmp_path)
645:     mirror = make_mirror(repo, tmp_path / "kv-t")
646:     bridge = make_bridge(repo, tmp_path / "kvs-t")
647:     for kwargs, label in (({"mirror": mirror}, "the reap mirror"),
648:                           ({"bridge": bridge}, "the reap bridge")):
649:         proc = attest(repo, base, head, **kwargs)
650:         assert proc.returncode == 2, label + ": " + proc.stdout + proc.stderr
651:         assert ("ERROR: " + label + " is not under the declared review mirror parent C:/pxm (") in proc.stdout, proc.stdout
652:         assert not att_file(repo, head).exists()
653:     assert mirror.exists() and bridge.exists()

656: def test_the_parent_itself_is_never_a_reap_path():
657:     # Source pin: the comparison is StartsWith on the parent WITH its
658:     # separator, never Equals, so `C:\pxm` can never be named as a tree.
659:     body = read(WRITE)
660:     guard = body[body.index("function Resolve-ReapPath"):body.index("function Invoke-Reap")]
661:     assert '$parentSlash = $mirrorParent.TrimEnd("/") + "/"' in guard
662:     assert "$p.StartsWith($parentSlash, $cmp)" in guard
663:     assert "$p.Equals($parentSlash, $cmp)" in guard
664:     # And it is the LAST rule: the head comparison precedes it.
665:     assert guard.index("not the attested head") < guard.index("not under the declared review mirror parent")

668: def test_an_unreadable_parent_refuses_every_reap(tmp_path, pxm):
669:     # The emitter reads the parent through artifact-roots.ps1, the one
670:     # reader of the declaration. A copy of the three tools beside a
671:     # doctored notes file whose mirror row has no placeholder cannot
672:     # resolve a parent, and a parent that cannot be read accepts
673:     # NOTHING: exit 2, record unwritten, tree untouched - even for a tree
674:     # that sits under the real parent.
675:     emitter = doctored_plugin(tmp_path, "Canonical review mirror root: `C:/pxm/`\n")
676:     repo, base, head = make_repo(tmp_path)
677:     mirror = make_mirror(repo, pxm / "kv-t")
678:     proc = run_ps(emitter,
679:                   "-RepoRoot", str(repo), "-BaseSha", base, "-HeadSha", head,
680:                   "-Verdict", "PASS", "-VerificationStatus", "FULL",
681:                   "-RouteNote", "effective route confirmed", "-Rounds", "1",
682:                   "-Participants", "session/reviewer", "-ReapMirror", str(mirror))
683:     assert proc.returncode == 2, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
684:     assert "ERROR: the declared review mirror parent could not be read" in proc.stdout, proc.stdout
685:     assert not att_file(repo, head).exists()
686:     assert mirror.exists()
687:     # Without a reap parameter the same doctored copy never reads the
688:     # parent and writes the record as before.
689:     proc = run_ps(emitter,
690:                   "-RepoRoot", str(repo), "-BaseSha", base, "-HeadSha", head,
691:                   "-Verdict", "PASS", "-VerificationStatus", "FULL",
692:                   "-RouteNote", "effective route confirmed", "-Rounds", "1",
693:                   "-Participants", "session/reviewer")
694:     assert proc.returncode == 0, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
695:     assert att_file(repo, head).is_file()

699:     # The other failure direction of the parent read: the doctored notes
700:     # have NO review mirror row, so artifact-roots.ps1 itself exits 2, and
701:     # the emitter reports that exit rather than treating an empty answer
702:     # as a parent. Record unwritten, tree untouched.
703:     emitter = doctored_plugin(tmp_path, "")
704:     repo, base, head = make_repo(tmp_path)
705:     mirror = make_mirror(repo, pxm / "kv-t")

707:                   "-RepoRoot", str(repo), "-BaseSha", base, "-HeadSha", head,
708:                   "-Verdict", "PASS", "-VerificationStatus", "FULL",
709:                   "-RouteNote", "effective route confirmed", "-Rounds", "1",
710:                   "-Participants", "session/reviewer", "-ReapMirror", str(mirror))
711:     assert proc.returncode == 2, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
712:     assert "ERROR: the declared review mirror parent could not be read: artifact-roots.ps1 exited 2" in proc.stdout, proc.stdout
713:     assert "Canonical review mirror root" in proc.stdout, proc.stdout

721: def test_the_record_lands_in_the_bracketed_repo_and_the_sibling_is_untouched(tmp_path, pxm):
722:     # Set-Content -Path expands wildcard characters, so a repo named
723:     # `repo[1]` with a matching plain-named sibling `repo1` could have its
724:     # record land in the sibling while the literal File.Exists read-back
725:     # accepted whatever old record already sat there. -LiteralPath plus a
726:     # content read-back closes that.
727:     repo, base, head = make_repo(tmp_path, name="repo[1]")
728:     sib = make_mirror(repo, tmp_path / "repo1")
729:     decoy_dir = sib / ".git" / "parallax" / "attestations"
730:     decoy_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
731:     decoy_file = decoy_dir / (head + ".json")
732:     decoy_bytes = json.dumps({"decoy": True}).encode("utf-8")
733:     decoy_file.write_bytes(decoy_bytes)
734:     # A stale record at the literal bracketed path: this is what e9d2713's
735:     # false-success branch would have accepted unread, since its File.Exists
736:     # check passed whatever record already sat there.
737:     att_file(repo, head).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
738:     att_file(repo, head).write_text(json.dumps({"stale": True}), encoding="utf-8")
739:     mirror = make_mirror(repo, pxm / "kv-t")
740:     proc = attest(repo, base, head, mirror=mirror)
741:     assert proc.returncode == 0, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
742:     assert att_file(repo, head).is_file()
743:     record = json.loads(att_file(repo, head).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
744:     assert record["head_sha"] == head
745:     assert "decoy" not in record
746:     assert "stale" not in record
747:     assert decoy_file.read_bytes() == decoy_bytes
748:     assert not mirror.exists()

328: # nothing outside it, so every tree a success case names is built under

1: # Mirror reaper design
2: 
3: Written 2026-09-13 from the KitnEssentials handoff
4: `dev/docs/handoffs/parallax-mirror-reaper-handoff.md` (outside this
5: repo). Backlog item 106 holds the measurement; item 98 is the paired
6: defect this design closes alongside.
7: 
8: ## The problem
9: 
10: Nothing in the plugin deletes a review mirror. On 2026-09-13 the drive
11: root held 78 `kv-*` / `kvs-*` directories totalling 13.4 GB, every one
12: created between 2026-09-09 and 2026-09-13 by multi-model-verify rounds:
13: about 3 GB per active review day. `kv-<tag>` is the review mirror the
14: plugin builds; `kvs-<tag>` is the drive-root clone bridge a session
15: builds first when the reviewed tree is a linked worktree, so the mirror
16: never copies a `.git` pointer file. The count also grew because the
17: mirror tool's existing-path refusal suggests `-Force`, and a session
18: that did not want to rebuild in place built `kv-<tag>-2` beside it.
19: 
20: ## The decision on the clone bridge
21: 
22: The plugin never created `kvs-*` and cannot recognise one by shape. Of
23: the three routes the handoff named:
24: 
25: - The mirror tool learning a `-SourceBridge` parameter would make it
26:   build clones, which its own header forbids for good reason (a clone
27:   carries tracked files only) and would move a KitnEssentials-only
28:   workaround into a project-agnostic tool. Rejected.
29: - A prose rule in the KitnEssentials memory alone is what exists today
30:   for every other artifact, and the measurement above is what a prose
31:   rule produces. Rejected as the only mechanism.
32: - **Chosen:** the attestation emitter accepts the bridge as an EXPLICIT
33:   argument, `-ReapBridge <path>`, beside `-ReapMirror <path>`. The
34:   session that built the bridge names it; the plugin removes what it is
35:   told under the same identity guard it applies to the mirror. The
36:   session-side rule "pass the bridge to the emitter" belongs in the
37:   KitnEssentials memory that describes the bridge, and this design
38:   records that as the consumer's follow-up rather than doing it here.
39: 
40: ## The reap point is the attestation
41: 
42: A round's `resume` re-verifies the mirror's identity, so a mirror
43: deleted mid-debate turns a resumable round into a transport failure.
44: The one terminal event the plugin already records mechanically is the
45: attestation, so `tools/write-attestation.ps1` is the reaper: it
46: validates every reap path BEFORE writing the record, writes the record,
47: then removes the trees. No time-based sweep exists anywhere, and the
48: doctor only reports.
49: 
50: Stated limit: a PLAN-mode debate builds the same mirror through preflight
51: step 3 and ends with a frozen plan, not an attestation, so its mirror has
52: no mechanical reap point. Its removal is the hand route the doctor names
53: until a plan-mode terminal event is recorded mechanically; backlog item
54: 107 carries that residual beside the same-head one.
55: 
56: ## The identity guard
57: 
58: A reap path is accepted only when ALL of these hold, checked before the
59: attestation is written so a refused argument costs nothing:
60: 
61: 1. It is rooted, resolves, and is not a filesystem root.
62: 2. It exists and is a directory.
63: 3. Neither it nor any existing ancestor is a reparse point (the mirror
64:    tool's own `Test-PathOrAncestorIsLink`, moved into a shared file and
65:    dot-sourced by both tools rather than copied).
66: 4. It is not equal to, inside, or containing the reviewed repository's
67:    top level or its git common dir (the mirror tool's own overlap
68:    comparisons, same spelling rule).
69: 5. Its `.git` entry is a DIRECTORY. A `.git` FILE is a linked worktree,
70:    which is never a mirror or a bridge, and deleting one corrupts the
71:    primary's worktree list.
72: 6. Its `HEAD` resolves to the attested head. For the mirror only, a
73:    HEAD whose author email is `parallax@local` and whose single parent
74:    is the attested head is also accepted, because that is the
75:    remediation commit the mirror tool makes over a tracked back-channel.
76:    A bridge has no such commit and must match exactly.
77: 
78: Rule 6 is what binds the reap to the debate that just ended: another
79: chat's mirror at another head is refused by name, and a bridge the
80: session forgot to fetch after a fix commit is refused rather than
81: deleted under a stale head.
82: 
83: ## The removal
84: 
85: One function, `Remove-ReviewTree`, in the shared file
86: `tools/review-tree-removal.ps1`, used by the emitter's reap and by the
87: mirror tool's `-Force` rebuild (item 98). It walks the tree itself in
88: post-order and never recurses through a reparse point: a directory link
89: is removed with `[System.IO.Directory]::Delete(link)` and a file link
90: with `[System.IO.File]::Delete(link)`, both of which remove the link and
91: never its target (measured 2026-09-13 on both hosts, intact and
92: dangling); an ordinary file has its read-only bit cleared first,
93: because git objects carry it; an ordinary directory is emptied then
94: removed with the non-recursive `Directory.Delete`, which throws when
95: anything survived. Every exception terminates the removal with the
96: entry's path in the reason. After the walk the root is re-examined and
97: must be gone. The function returns `@{ Ok = $true }` or
98: `@{ Ok = $false; Reason = <text> }`; it never exits the caller.
99: 
100: The mirror's `<mirror>.source-manifest` sibling, when it exists as an
101: ordinary file, is removed after the mirror.
102: 
103: ## Exit codes and messages
104: 
105: - Emitter: `0` written (and reaped when asked); `2` argument or repo
106:   error, which now includes every reap-path refusal, made before the
107:   record is written; `3` the attestation was written and a reap failed,
108:   named on stdout as `ERROR: reap failed for <path>: <reason>`, with
109:   the record left standing.
110: - Mirror tool: an existing `-MirrorPath` without `-Force` is refused
111:   with a message that names `write-attestation.ps1 -ReapMirror` as the
112:   route for a finished debate and `-Force` as the in-place rebuild for
113:   a live one. A `-Force` removal that fails terminates with
114:   `ERROR: the existing mirror could not be removed: <reason>` and exit

385: that is `C:\pxm`. List every DIRECTORY directly under that parent.
386: Measure the count, the total size in GB (sum of file lengths, reparse
387: points NOT followed), and the oldest `LastWriteTime` among them. A
388: directory that cannot be measured is named as unmeasured and counted;
389: it never reads as empty. A parent that does not exist is OK with
390: `no review mirrors present`; a resolver that exits non-zero is BROKEN
391: with its `ERROR:` line, never an empty inventory.
392: 
393: - Nothing found: OK, `no review mirrors present`.
394: - Found, total under 5 GB AND oldest under 3 days: OK, reported as a
395:   NOTE with the three numbers.
396: - Total 5 GB or more, OR oldest 3 days or more: STALE, with the three
397:   numbers and this fix: a finished debate's mirror is removed by the
398:   attestation emitter, `write-attestation.ps1 -ReapMirror <mirror>
399:   [-ReapBridge <bridge>]`, which accepts only a tree under the declared
400:   parent, and one whose debate is over without an attestation is
401:   removed by hand; the rule and its measurement are backlog item 106,
402:   the parent is backlog item 107. Never name a directory as safe to
403:   delete: the doctor cannot tell which of them a live chat can still
404:   resume, and a `resume` against a deleted mirror is a transport
405:   failure.
